Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a geometry algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 481. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 181/3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by geometry generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 481, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 181. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 216.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on geometry structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 621. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 766 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the optimality condition condition on geometry structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 621. The convergence follows from the optimality condition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 766. QED.
